- **prompt-improve**: Run the deterministic linter and rewrite engine on the ask the user supplies with this command and return their outputs verbatim. Mode defaults to `rewrite`; the user may name `advisory` (score the raw ask only, no rewrite), `rewrite_and_handoff` (execute afterwards only when the linter grants eligibility), or `rewrite_llm` (adds a gate-checked, advisory LLM-polished variant — SPEC §23). This comm ([ancyonio/PromptIQ](https://github.com/ancyonio/PromptIQ/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/prompt-improve.md) · ★1)
- **prompt-improve**: Rewrite a rough ask into a structured task spec using the deterministic PromptIQ engine, scored by the deterministic linter. Modes: advisory (score only), rewrite (default), rewrite_llm (gated advisory polish), rewrite_and_handoff (linter-gated). ([ancyonio/PromptIQ](https://github.com/ancyonio/PromptIQ/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/prompt-improve.md) · ★1)
